@#16

EN: Sweets trigger opiate-like beta-endorphin, then dopamine, stimulating appetite. Orexin follows, relaxing the stomach and moving food into the small intestine to aid digestion. That's how we make room for dessert!

@#17

EN: Highlanders have more red blood cells, helping them cope with low oxygen. But after a stay in the lowlands, even they may get altitude sickness on returning home.

@#24

EN: Deserts have phantom rivers called wadis.<n>Wadi is an Arabic word for river.<n>They normally have no flowing water.<n>But a desert can get a whole year's rain in one day,<n>and suddenly a wadi becomes a real river.
